ElementTree uses '' for default namespace\n        if prefix in ns:\n            continue\n        ns[prefix] = uri\n        ET.register_namespace(prefix if prefix is not None else \"\", uri)\n    return ns\n\n\ndef localname(tag: str) -> str:\n    if tag.startswith(\"{\"):\n        return tag.split(\"}\", 1)[1]\n    return tag\n\n\ndef get_paragraph_text(p_elem, nsmap):\n    # Gather all hp:t descendants in document order\n    texts = []\n    for t in p_elem.findall(\".//hp:t\", nsmap):\n        if t.text:\n            texts.append(t.text)\n    return \"\".join(texts)\n\n\ndef set_paragraph_text_flat(p_elem, nsmap, new_text: str):\n    \"\"\"Replace paragraph's textual content with a single run/t, keeping para attributes.\"\"\"\n    # Find first run (direct child) if exists; else create one.\n    run = None\n    for child in list(p_elem):\n        if child.tag == f\"{{{nsmap['hp']}}}run\":\n            run = child\n            break\n    if run is None:\n        run = ET.SubElement(p_elem, f\"{{{nsmap['hp']}}}run\")\n        run.set(\"charPrIDRef\", \"0\")\n\n    # Remove all runs after the first (they would contribute additional text)\n    for child in list(p_elem):\n        if child.tag == f\"{{{nsmap['hp']}}}run\" and child is not run:\n            p_elem.remove(child)\n\n    # Clear the kept run's children then set a single hp:t\n    for rc in list(run):\n        run.remove(rc)\n    t = ET.SubElement(run, f\"{{{nsmap['hp']}}}t\")\n    t.text = new_text\n\n\ndef remove_layout_cache_for_paragraph(p_elem):\n    \"\"\"Remove cached layout elements (linesegarray variants) within a paragraph.\"\"\"\n    # Build parent map for removals\n    parent_map = {c: p for p in p_elem.iter() for c in p}\n    to_remove = []\n    for elem in p_elem.iter():\n        ln = localname(elem.tag).lower()\n        # The task calls out linesegarray/lineSegArray/variants\n        if ln.endswith(\"linesegarray\") or ln == \"linesegarray\":\n            to_remove.append(elem)\n    # Remove deepest-first so parent removals don't break traversal\n    for elem in reversed(to_remove):\n        parent = parent_map.get(elem)\n        if parent is not None:\n            parent.remove(elem)\n\n\ndef update_section_xml(xml_bytes: bytes, replacements, followup_items):\n    ns = collect_and_register_namespaces(xml_bytes)\n    # Expecting 'hp' prefix in these documents\n    hp_uri = ns.get(\"hp\") or \"http://www.hancom.co.kr/hwpml/2010/HWPML\"\n    nsmap = {\"hp\": hp_uri}\n\n    root = ET.fromstring(xml_bytes)\n\n    modified_any = False\n\n    # --- Text replacements across paragraphs ---\n    for p in root.findall(\".//hp:p\", nsmap):\n        old_text = get_paragraph_text(p, nsmap)\n        if not old_text:\n            continue\n\n        # Preserve appendix sentence exactly (never touch that paragraph)\n        if \"이 부록 문단은 그대로 유지해야 합니다.\" in old_text:\n            continue\n\n        new_text = old_text\n        for old, new in replacements:\n            if old in new_text:\n                new_text = new_text.replace(old, new)\n\n        if new_text != old_text:\n            set_paragraph_text_flat(p, nsmap, new_text)\n            remove_layout_cache_for_paragraph(p)\n            modified_any = True\n\n    # --- Follow-up line replacement / resizing (section that contains them) ---\n    # Identify follow-up paragraphs by numbering pattern \"N. ...\"\n    numbered_paras = []\n    for p in root.findall(\".//hp:p\", nsmap):\n        txt = get_paragraph_text(p, nsmap).strip()\n        if re.match(r\"^\\d+\\.\\s\", txt):\n            numbered_paras.append((p, txt))\n\n    if numbered_paras:\n        # Use the first contiguous block starting at 1.\n        # This avoids accidentally touching other numbered lists elsewhere.\n        block = []\n        expected = 1\n        for p, txt in numbered_paras:\n            m = re.match(r\"^(\\d+)\\.\\s\", txt)\n            if not m:\n                continue\n            num = int(m.group(1))\n            if not block:\n                if num != 1:\n                    continue\n                block.append(p)\n                expected = 2\n                continue\n            if num == expected:\n                block.append(p)\n                expected += 1\n            else:\n                break\n\n        if block:\n            parent_map = {c: par for par in root.iter() for c in par}\n            parent = parent_map.get(block[0])\n\n            # Determine insertion point (index after last existing follow-up p)\n            last_p = block[-1]\n            insert_at = list(parent).index(last_p) + 1 if parent is not None else None\n\n            # Determine max paragraph id for new ones\n            max_id = -1\n            for p in root.findall(\".//hp:p\", nsmap):\n                pid = p.get(\"id\")\n                if pid is not None and pid.isdigit():\n                    max_id = max(max_id, int(pid))\n\n            # Clear text now; we'll set below\n                    set_paragraph_text_flat(new_p, nsmap, \"\")\n                    remove_layout_cache_for_paragraph(new_p)\n                    parent.insert(insert_at, new_p)\n                    insert_at += 1\n                    block.append(new_p)\n\n            if target_n < cur_n and parent is not None:\n                for extra_p in block[target_n:]:\n                    parent.remove(extra_p)\n                block = block[:target_n]\n\n            # Now set texts\n            for idx, (p, item) in enumerate(zip(block, followup_items), start=1):\n                desired = f\"{idx}. {item}\"\n                existing = get_paragraph_text(p, nsmap)\n                if existing != desired:\n                    set_paragraph_text_flat(p, nsmap, desired)\n                    remove_layout_cache_for_paragraph(p)\n                    modified_any = True\n\n    out = io.BytesIO()\n    tree = ET.ElementTree(root)\n    tree.write(out, encoding=\"UTF-8\", xml_declaration=True)\n    return out.getvalue(), modified_any\n\n\ndef main():\n    original_hwpx = \"/tmp/hwpx_work/original.hwpx\"\n    out_path = \"/root/renewal_playbook_updated.hwpx\"\n\n    # Remove deepest-first so parent removals don't break traversal\n    for elem in reversed(to_remove):\n        parent = parent_map.get(elem)\n        if parent is not None:\n            parent.remove(elem)\n\n\ndef update_section_xml(xml_bytes: bytes, replacements, followup_items):\n    ns = collect_and_register_namespaces(xml_bytes)\n    # Expecting 'hp' prefix in these documents\n    hp_uri = ns.get(\"hp\") or \"http://www.hancom.co.kr/hwpml/2010/HWPML\"\n    nsmap = {\"hp\": hp_uri}\n\n    root = ET.fromstring(xml_bytes)\n\n    modified_any = False\n\n    # --- Follow-up line replacement / resizing (section that contains them) ---\n    # Identify follow-up paragraphs by numbering pattern \"N. ...\"\n    numbered_paras = []\n    for p in root.findall(\".//hp:p\", nsmap):\n        txt = get_paragraph_text(p, nsmap).strip()\n        if re.match(r\"^\\d+\\.\\s\", txt):\n            numbered_paras.append((p, txt))\n\n    if numbered_paras:\n        # Use the first contiguous block starting at 1.\n        # This avoids accidentally touching other numbered lists elsewhere.\n        block = []\n        expected = 1\n        for p, txt in numbered_paras:\n            m = re.match(r\"^(\\d+)\\.\\s\", txt)\n            if not m:\n                continue\n            num = int(m.group(1))\n            if not block:\n                if num != 1:\n                    continue\n                block.append(p)\n                expected = 2\n                continue\n            if num == expected:\n                block.append(p)\n                expected += 1\n            else:\n                break\n\n        if block:\n            parent_map = {c: par for par in root.iter() for c in par}\n            parent = parent_map.get(block[0])\n\n            # Determine insertion point (index after last existing follow-up p)\n            last_p = block[-1]\n            insert_at = list(parent).index(last_p) + 1 if parent is not None else None\n\n            # Determine max paragraph id for new ones\n            max_id = -1\n            for p in root.findall(\".//hp:p\", nsmap):\n                pid = p.get(\"id\")\n                if pid is not None and pid.isdigit():\n                    max_id = max(max_id, int(pid))\n\n            # Expand or shrink block to match number of follow-up items\n            target_n = len(followup_items)\n            cur_n = len(block)\n\n            if target_n > cur_n and parent is not None:\n                template = block[-1]\n                for _ in range(target_n - cur_n):\n                    max_id += 1\n                    new_p = copy.deepcopy(template)\n                    new_p.set(\"id\", str(max_id))\n                    # Clear text now; we'll set below\n                    set_paragraph_text_flat(new_p, nsmap, \"\")\n                    remove_layout_cache_for_paragraph(new_p)\n                    parent.insert(insert_at, new_p)\n                    insert_at += 1\n                    block.append(new_p)\n\n            if target_n < cur_n and parent is not None:\n                for extra_p in block[target_n:]:\n                    parent.remove(extra_p)\n                block = block[:target_n]\n\n            #
